elements\")\n        fmin = _positive_finite(value[0], field_name=\"freq_range[0]\")\n        fmax = _positive_finite(value[1], field_name=\"freq_range[1]\")\n        if fmax <= fmin:\n            raise ValueError(\"freq_range[1] must be greater than freq_range[0]\")\n        return (fmin, fmax)\n\n    @property\n    def freq0(self) -> float:\n        return 0.5 * (self.freq_range[0] + self.freq_range[1])\n\n    @property\n    def bandwidth(self) -> float:\n        return self.freq_range[1] - self.freq_range[0]\n\n    @property\n    def fwidth(self) -> float:\n        half_range = 0.5 * self.bandwidth\n        return half_range / math.sqrt(2.0 * math.log(1.0 / self.minimum_amplitude))\n\n    @property\n    def twidth(self) -> float:\n        return 1.0 / (2.0 * math.pi * self.fwidth)\n\n    @property\n    def automatic_delay(self) -> float:\n        return 5.0 * self.twidth\n\n    @property\n    def offset_time(self) -> float:\n        range_twidth = 1.0 / (2.0 * math.pi * self.bandwidth)\n        return self.automatic_delay + self.offset * range_twidth\n\n    @property\n    def peak_time(self) -> float:\n        return self.offset_time\n\n    def amp_time(self, time: float | Sequence[float]) -> np.ndarray:\n        times = _normalize_times(time)\n        omega0 = 2.0 * math.pi * self.freq0\n        shifted = times - self.offset_time\n        return (\n            1j\n            * np.exp(1j * self.phase)\n            * np.exp(-1j * omega0 * times)\n            * np.exp(-(shifted**2) / (2.0 * self.twidth**2))\n            * self.amplitude\n        )\n\n    def amp_freq(self, freq: float | Sequence[float]) -> np.ndarray:\n        freqs = np.asarray(freq, dtype=float)\n        if freqs.ndim == 0:\n            freqs = freqs.reshape(1)\n        if freqs.ndim != 1 or not np.all(np.isfinite(freqs)):\n            raise ValueError(\"frequency samples must be a scalar or one-dimensional finite sequence\")\n        spread = np.exp(-((freqs - self.freq0) ** 2) / (2.0 * self.fwidth**2))\n        return self.amplitude * np.exp(1j * self.phase) * spread\n\n    def frequency_range_sigma(self, sigma: float = DEFAULT_SIGMA) -> tuple[float, float]:\n        sigma_value = _positive_finite(sigma, field_name=\"sigma\")\n        return (\n            max(0.0, self.freq0 - sigma_value * self.fwidth),\n            self.freq0 + sigma_value * self.fwidth,\n        )\n\n    def frequency_range(self, num_fwidth: float = DEFAULT_SIGMA) -> tuple[float, float]:\n        return self.frequency_range_sigma(sigma=num_fwidth)\n\n    def end_time(self) -> float:\n        return self.offset_time + END_TIME_FACTOR_GAUSSIAN * self.twidth\n\n\nSourceTimeModel = GaussianPulse | ContinuousWave | BroadbandPulse | centers=(float(centers[lower]),),\n            )\n        left_center = centers[lower]\n        right_center = centers[upper]\n        fraction = float((center - left_center) / (right_center - left_center))\n        return CompiledAxisPlacement(\n            indices=(lower, upper),\n            weights=(1.0 - fraction, fraction),\n            cell_sizes=(float(cell_sizes[lower]), float(cell_sizes[upper])),\n            centers=(float(left_center), float(right_center)),\n        )\n\n    lower = center - size / 2.0\n    upper = center + size / 2.0\n    if confine_to_bounds:\n        overlaps = np.minimum(boundaries[1:], upper) - np.maximum(boundaries[:-1], lower)\n        mask = overlaps > tolerance\n        if not np.any(mask):\n            raise ValueError(\"UniformCurrentSource support does not overlap the resolved grid\")\n        weights = overlaps[mask] / cell_sizes[mask]\n        indices = np.nonzero(mask)[0]\n    else:\n        mask = (centers >= lower - tolerance) & (centers <= upper + tolerance)\n        if not np.any(mask):\n            raise ValueError(\"UniformCurrentSource support does not include any resolved grid cells\")\n        weights = np.ones(int(np.count_nonzero(mask)), dtype=float)\n        indices = np.nonzero(mask)[0]\n    return CompiledAxisPlacement(\n        indices=tuple(int(index) for index in indices),\n        weights=tuple(float(weight) for weight in weights),\n        cell_sizes=tuple(float(cell_sizes[index]) for index in indices),\n        centers=tuple(float(centers[index]) for index in indices),\n    )\n\n\ndef _transverse_extent(source: UniformCurrentSource, grid: ResolvedGrid) -> float:\n    placements = []\n    axes = (grid.x, grid.y, grid.z)\n    for axis in range(3):\n        if axis == source.component_axis:\n            continue\n        extent = source.size[axis]\n        if extent > 0.0:\n            placements.append(extent)\n            continue\n        centers = measure = 1.0\n    for extent in placements:\n        measure *= extent\n    return measure\n\n\ndef compile_uniform_current_source(\n    source: UniformCurrentSource,\n    *,\n    grid: confine_to_bounds=source.confine_to_bounds,\n        )\n        for axis, axis_grid in enumerate((grid.x, grid.y, grid.z))\n    )\n\n    placements: list[tuple[int, int, int]] = []\n    placement_weights: list[float] = []\n    for x_index, x_weight in zip(\n        axis_placements[0].indices,\n        axis_placements[0].weights,\n        strict=True,\n    ):\n        for y_index, y_weight in zip(\n            axis_placements[1].indices,\n            axis_placements[1].weights,\n            strict=True,\n        ):\n            for z_index, z_weight in zip(\n                axis_placements[2].indices,\n                axis_placements[2].weights,\n                strict=True,\n            ):\n                placements.append((x_index, y_index, z_index))\n                placement_weights.append(float(x_weight * y_weight * z_weight))\n\n    amplitude_scale = 1.0\n    if source.current_amplitude_definition == \"total\":\n        transverse_extent = _transverse_extent(source, grid)\n        if transverse_extent <= 0.0:\n            raise ValueError(\"UniformCurrentSource total-current normalization needs positive extent\")\n        amplitude_scale = 1.0 / transverse_extent\n\n    return CompiledUniformCurrentSource(\n        source=source,\n        field_kind=source.field_kind,\n        component_axis=source.component_axis,\n        placement_kind=source.placement_kind,\n        placements=tuple(placements),\n        placement_weights=tuple(placement_weights),\n        axis_placements=axis_placements,\n The source corresponds to an infinitesimal antenna\n    with a fixed current density, and is slightly different from a related definition that is used\n    in some contexts, namely an oscillating electric or magnetic dipole. The two are related through\n    a factor of ``omega ** 2`` in the power normalization, where ``omega`` is the angular frequency\n    of the oscillation. time-dependence.\n        angle_theta : float\n            Polar angle w.r.t. the z-axis.\n        angle_phi : float\n            Azimuth angle around the z-axis.\n        component : Literal[\"electric\", \"magnetic\"] = \"electric\"\n            The type of polarization.\n        kwargs : dict\n            Keyword arguments passed to ``PointDipole()``, excluding ``source_time`` and ``polarization``\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        list[PointDipole]\n            A list of ``PointDipole`` objects that emulate a single dipole with an arbitrary direction of polarization.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not (component == \"electric\" or component == \"magnetic\"):\n            raise ValueError('Component must be \"electric\" or \"magnetic\"')\n\n        dipoles: list[PointDipole] = []\n        polarizations = [\"Ex\", \"Ey\", \"Ez\"] if component == \"electric\" else [\"Hx\", \"Hy\", \"Hz\"]\n\n        multipliers = [\n            sin(angle_theta) * cos(angle_phi),\n            sin(angle_theta) * sin(angle_phi),\n            cos(angle_theta),\n        ]\n\n        for polarization, mult in zip(polarizations, multipliers):\n            if not ``E`` and ``H`` fields.\n\n    Notes\n    -----\n\n        Injects the specified components of the ``E`` and ``H`` dataset directly as ``J`` and ``M`` current\n        distributions in the FDTD solver. The coordinates of all provided fields are assumed to be relative to the\n        source center.\n        In other words, the dataset is interpreted in a local coordinate frame centered at\n        :attr:`center`; when injecting/interpolating, the simulation-space coordinates are\n        ``dataset_coords + center``. This means the same dataset can be translated in space by\n        changing :attr:`center` without modifying dataset coordinates.\n\n        The syntax is very similar to :class:`CustomFieldSource`, except instead of a ``field_dataset``, the source\n        accepts a :attr:`current_dataset`. This dataset still contains :math:`E_{x,y,z}` and :math:`H_{x,y,\n        z}` field components, which correspond to :math:`J` and :math:`M` components respectively. There are also\n        fewer constraints on the data requirements for :class:`CustomCurrentSource`. It can be volumetric or planar\n        without requiring tangential components. Finally, note that the dataset is still defined w.r.t. the source\n        center, just as in the case of the :class:`CustomFieldSource`, and can then be placed anywhere in the simulation.\n\n    Example\n    -------\n    >>> from tidy3d import ScalarFieldDataArray, GaussianPulse\n    >>> import numpy as np\n    >>> pulse = GaussianPulse(freq0=200e12, fwidth=20e12)\n    >>> x = np.linspace(-1,    def _confine_mask(self, field_data: ScalarFieldDataArray) -> np.ndarray:\n        \"\"\"Mask selecting dataset points inside source bounds on nonzero-size axes.\"\"\"\n        mask = np.ones(field_data.shape, dtype=float)\n        for dim in \"xyz\":\n            if dim not in field_data.coords:\n                continue\n            axis = \"xyz\".index(dim)\n            half_size = 0.5 * float(self.size[axis])\n            if half_size <= 0.0:\n                continue\n            coords = np.asarray(field_data.coords[dim].data, dtype=float)\n            inside = np.abs(coords) <= (half_size + 1e-12)\n            reshape = [1] * mask.ndim\n            reshape[field_data.dims.index(dim)] = coords.size\n            mask *= inside.reshape(reshape)\n        return mask\n\n    def _adjoint_interp_methods(self) -> dict[str, str]:\n        \"\"\"Interpolation mode per axis for source VJP projection.\"\"\"\n        if self.interpolate:\n            return \"linear\"\n        return axis_methods\n\n    def _compute_dataset_derivatives(\n        self,\n        dataset_paths: list[tuple],\n        *,\n        center: tuple[float, float, float],\n
